Used to introduce direct or indirect questions about time."],"tags":["interrogative","not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The site's all bugged. Fix when?","type":"example"},{"text":"Tank class buff when?","type":"example"},{"text":"My fridge even restocks itself these days. Used to introduce direct or indirect questions about time.","Used after a noun or noun phrase in isolation to express impatience with an anticipated future event."],"tags":["Internet","humorous","interrogative","not-comparable","often"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He's mister high and mighty now, but I remember him when.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At an earlier time and under different, usually less favorable, circumstances."],"tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"That was the day when the Twin Towers fell.","type":"example"},{"text":"Not a week goes by when I don't mourn that loss.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At which, on which, during which: often omitted or replaced with that."],"tags":["not-comparable","relative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"I recall when they were called the Greys.","type":"example"},{"text":"Next year is when we elect a new mayor.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The time at, on or during which."],"qualifier":"fused relative","tags":["not-comparable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Love is when you can't get enough of someone.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A circumstance or situation in which."],"raw_tags":["in definitions or explanations"],"tags":["informal","not-comparable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/wɛn/"},{"ipa":"/wɪn/","tags":["pin-pen-merger"]},{"ipa":"/ʍɛn/"},{"ipa":"/w̥ɛn/"},{"ipa":"/ʍɪn/"},{"ipa":"/w̥ɪn/","tags":["pin-pen-merger"]}],"upstream_line":32830,"upstream_offset":223315497,"upstream_record_sha256":"fb0876e6d1c52860933ba2bb4f3674ddc02016bd8da4e2db097f3d7237063464","word":"when"}
{"forms":[{"form":"wen","tags":["alternative","pronunciation-spelling"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"conj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Pavlov's dogs salivate when [i.e. at any and every time that] they hear a bell.","type":"example"},{"text":"When [i.e. at any and every time that] he speaks to her, he is always polite.","type":"example"},{"text":"Put your pencil down when [i.e. as soon as, at the moment that] the timer goes off.","type":"example"},{"text":"A player wins when [as soon as, or at any time that, if] she has four cards of the same suit.","type":"example"},{"text":"A student is disqualified when [as soon as, if] they cheat.","type":"example"},{"text":"Don't forget to signal when (you are) turning right.","type":"example"},{"text":"When she'd written her letters, she did some gardening.","type":"example"},{"text":"Jose, when he finally managed to go to sleep, had a series of bad dreams.","type":"example"},{"text":"I said nothing about doing that when I had the time.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At (or as soon as) that time that; at the (or any and every) time that; if."]},{"examples":[{"text":"They dream when [i.e. during the time that] they sleep.","type":"example"},{"text":"I'm happiest when [during the time that, or at any time that] I’m working.","type":"example"},{"text":"It was raining when I came yesterday.","type":"example"},{"text":"The game is over when the referee says it is.","type":"example"},{"text":"Be careful when crossing the street.","type":"example"},{"text":"When (you are) angry, count to ten before speaking or acting.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["During the time that; at the time of the action of the following clause or participle phrase."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I am here till Friday, when [i.e. at which time] I leave for Senegal.","type":"example"},{"text":"I was just walking down the street, when [i.e. at which time] all of a sudden it started to rain.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["At what time; at which time."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I don't see the point of putting up Christmas decorations when I am the only person who is going to see them.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Since; given the fact that; considering that."]},{"examples":[{"text":"You're picking at your scabs when you should be letting them heal.","type":"example"},{"text":"He keeps changing things when the existing system works perfectly well.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Whereas; although; at the same time as; in spite of the fact that."]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/wɛn/"},{"ipa":"/wɪn/","tags":["pin-pen-merger"]},{"ipa":"/ʍɛn/"},{"ipa":"/w̥ɛn/"},{"ipa":"/ʍɪn/"},{"ipa":"/w̥ɪn/","tags":["pin-pen-merger"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"as soon as","word":"as soon as"},{"sense":"as soon as","word":"immediately"},{"sense":"as soon as","word":"once"},{"sense":"every time that","word":"whenever"},{"sense":"during the time that","word":"while"},{"word":"whilst"},{"sense":"at any time that","word":"whenever"},{"sense":"given the fact that","word":"given that"},{"word":"seeing that"},{"sense":"in spite of the fact that","word":"but"},{"sense":"in spite of the fact that","word":"where"},{"sense":"in spite of the fact that","word":"whereas"}],"upstream_line":32831,"upstream_offset":223380950,"upstream_record_sha256":"8206860349c70a4e0b37a64ce47a9ed25fe819b89f17f7113983b6ba1252666e","word":"when"}
